				limit = { has_selected_land_grand_doctrine = yes }

					amount = 15
					folder = land
				}

				army_experience = 15
			}
			add_command_power = 20
		}

			base = 80
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_active_mission = bankruptcy_incoming_collapse }
		}
